  "interpretation": {
    "blockedBySampledTerrain": "The target's apparent elevation is at or below the calculated terrain horizon at its azimuth.",
    "aboveSampledTerrain": "The target is above the sampled terrain horizon, but visibility is not confirmed because complete horizon coverage has not been verified.",
    "visibilityWindows": "Windows represent sampled observation intervals. Their endpoints are not exact rise or set times.",
    "unavailableEphemeris": "No target visibility assessment is available when its JPL Horizons ephemeris request fails."
  },
  "notes": [
    "Ephemeris positions are obtained from the JPL Horizons API.",
    "Sun and Earth requests are handled independently.",
    "Temporary JPL service failures are retried up to three times.",
    "Terrain analysis is returned independently of ephemeris availability.",
    "The observer is located at the Site04 Shackleton Rim coordinates.",
    "Terrain obstruction is evaluated using the calculated LOLA terrain horizon.",
    "Product 78 provides the local 5-metre terrain model.",
    "Product 90 provides wider regional terrain coverage at 20-metre resolution.",
    "A target above the sampled terrain horizon is not automatically classified as confirmed visible.",
    "The terrain horizon is not considered complete until its coverage limitations have been resolved.",
    "Visibility windows use 10-minute samples and do not calculate exact transition times.",
    "Unavailable ephemeris data is not estimated or fabricated."
  ]
}
